kiba wan na shatci bad don pat kiba wan na shathie jong ka ri 
Wales. Ki wan ruh na Merseyside bad Manchester, ha kaba 
kiba bun ki Balang jong kine ki shnong ki la ai mon sngewbha 
ia ki brie .V shynrang bad kynthei bad ruh kiba la ai kylluid ki

katne ki briew ha ri India kiba la ioh pdiang ia ki jingai ba bha 
haduh ba ki la ngeit ba ka Liverpool ka long ka nongbah jong 
ka ri V/alcs. 

Kine ki khyndai ngut ki mishonari kum kiwei pat ki para 
mishonari jong ki ki wan na nongkyndong bad na sor bad ka 
ktien Welsh ka long ka kticn tynrai jong ki. Ki la leil sha ki jaka 
kyndong bad ki jaka ki bym da ia tip paidbah ha ki bynta jong 
ka India kaba Shatei Lam Shatei Mihngi kaba la tip da ka 
kyrteng Assam ha kito ki sngi. Kine ki donkam ban hikai ha ki 
skul bad ki college, ki donkam ban ai dawai ha ki hospital bad 
ki jaka ai dawai bad ki donkam ban iaiap ha ki ia ka Gospel; 
baroh kane ka long ha ka ktien kaba ia pher na ka ktien tynrai 
jong ki. Kine suki suki ki la pyniadei ia lade bad kawei pat ka 
kolshor; ka kolshor Bengali ha ri them bad ka kolshor jong ki 



Tribal Mongolian ha ri lum. Kane ka long ka bym lah lait ia ki 
namar ki la dei ban shong ban sah la kumno kumno san snem 
lane kham bunsien hynhiew snem ha kita ki ri kiha ki la jicd 
ban leit haduh ka por wan shuti jong ki, hynrei ka la long kaba 
myntoi pynban namar ki la long ka bynta jong ka jingim jong 
kito ki briew kiba ki la shakri. Na kine ki jingthoh yn iohi ba 
bun na ki ki la shong la sah ha ka ri kaba ki ki trei hapdeng 
laiphew bad sawphew snem. I Miss Annie Thomas da ka 
jingkyntait jong i ban wan phai bad ban shongthait sha ri Bilat i 
la pynlut ia ki 66 snem ha India. Lehse i la long iba stad. Ka 
jingshongthait noh na ka kam, ia kiba bun ki mishonari ka 
pynlong pynban ka por jong ka jingbym shongsuk namar ki 
shorn pynban ba ki long tang ki nongwei ha la ka jong ka ri bad 
kim lah shuh ban leit khmih pat ia ka ri ha kaba ki la pynlut ia 
ka por kaba bun jong ka jingim jong ki. 

Kine ki khyndai ngut ki ai ia ka dur kaba biang ia ka 
jingtrei ba la maitphang da ka Balang Presbyterian jong ka Ri 
Wales ha kiba bun ki bynta jong ka Ri India. Ki la trei ha ka 
rukom trei ’’kaba rim" kaba dei ka jingtrei ha ki ri sha shiliang 
duriaw, kaba haba khmih mynta i kumba ka long ka rukom trei 
ki riew rim ha kaba pynphriang ia ka Gospel, pynkhiah ia kiba 
pang, kaba seng ia ki skul hangno gangno haba i don lad

jingaiei U Khrist, ka jingieit jong ki na ka bynta U Blei bad ka 
jingieit kaba jylliew ia kito ki briew la jong ka la pynmih ia ki 
syntiew jong ka Gospel ha kiba bun ki jaka kiba kyndong ki 
bym da paw pyrthei ha kata ka ri kaba khraw ka Ri India.

U REV EDWIN ROWLANDS 

1867-1939 


Nga don ka dur jong u ba la shon nga tharai ha ka 1902 
haba u la dei khmih ia ka wSkuI Mishon ha Ai/awi. U phong ka

Khristan ki la kot haduh % ngut na ka shispah.

tupia na shnong Dallas ! 

U la Icit sha America haba u dang khynnah bad u la trei

Wales ban pynkhreh ia lade na ka bynta ka lyngkha mishon ha 
ka jaka ka ban kham shyrkhei ban ia ka Texas.

iaid briew ban wad da ki lynti ba lyngkot na uwei u syntai lum 
sha uwei pat - hynrei um shym ia lah leh ei ei. Ka la shim katto 
katne por ban pynsngewthuh ia u ba ki Mizo bad ki mrad khlaw 
ki la lah shem ia ki lynti khlaw baroh lyngba kita ki them.

Aizawl, don ka bynta kaba don ki khla kiba runar. Ki nongtrei

ryngkat bad ki traishnong ki ju sumar bha ban ym mih shabar 
shuwa ban dum. Uwei u nonghikai kata ka shnong u la iathuh

iaid marwei ha kawei ka miet tang bad ka lynten ha ka kti jong 
u. Ha kata ka khlaw kaba rben u la pynlut bun kynta ban wad ia 
uwei u khynnah uba jah na i'ing haduh ba un da shem ia u. 

U long u nongthaw jingrwai uba nang bad kiba bun na ki

thaw da u. U tbit bha ruh ban pynkylla ktien na ka Phareng bad

I MISS ANNIE W. THOMAS 

1871 -1963 


Ka long ka kabu bad jingsngewbha kaba khraw ban thoh 
shaphang iwei iba la aiti ia la ka jingim ban shakri ia la U Trai 
ha ki lum jong ka Assam - i Miss Annie W. Thomas. 

La kha ia i Miss Thomas ha ka 4 tarik u June 1871. 1 long 
ka khun pdeng jong u Rev. John Thomas, 6.A. uba long shipor 
u pastor jong ka Balang Hope, Merthyr, bad hadien ha 
Catharine Street, Liverpool. La la kha ia i Miss Thomas ha 
Merthyr, i la mih noh dang rit, bad i la pyniajan ialade bad ka 
Liverpool, bad khamtam bad ka Balang ha Catharine Street. 
Bunsien i ju iaiap ia kane ka Balang da ka jingsngewieit kaba 
khraw. 

I la pule ha Windsor High School kaba sha rudnong jong 
ka Liverpool, bad kumba katto katne snem i la trei kum ka 
nonghikai ha kata ka skuL 

Ha ki por ba lait ei, i la pule ha ka School of Art, bad ia 
ki jingdro dur jong i la ju pyni kai hateng hateng. I la 
pyndqnkam ia kane ka sap ha ri India, bad bun napdeng jong 
ngi Ja ju iai pynkynmaw ia ki ling ki sem jong ngi ha India 
namar ki jingdro dur ba la dro da i Miss Thomas. La 
pyndonkam ia kane ka sap ruh ha ki Skul Pynpaw bad ki 
Jingiaseng Iaiap. Ym tang ba i lah ban iathuh ia ki 
Jingiathuhkhana na ka Baibl, hynrei ban pynshai da ki dur jong 
i hi ba la pyndonkam kum ki Flannelgraph. 

La pynsan pynrangbah ia i Miss Thomas ha ka ung 


52 



Khristan ba riewblei ha Liverpool, ka jaka pdeng jong ka 
mishon, ka Falkner Street, ka ophis mishon ka long tang 
khyndiat sien jam na ka ungmane - kam long kaba sngewphylla 
ba ha ka karta ba dang khadar snem, ka la jia long ka jing- 
angnud jong i ban shakri ia U Trai ha Assam, India. 

Ka la long ha u bnai October, 1897 ba i lamihnala 
ling ban pyndep ia kane ka jingthrang, bad i la ai ia ki hynriew- 
phew hynriew sncm jong ka jingshakri mon sngewbha. I la 
pynlut shiteng na ka rta ba ka mishon ka la don ha ki ri lum 
jong ka Assam, kata, haduh ka snem 1963. 

Nga la ju iohsngew bunsien ba i ju iathuh ia ki 
jingiashem jong i - ban iehnoh ta ka Ving basuk basaih, ha kaba 
la ri la sumar ieit ta i. I long iba rit ha ka met ka phad, i bym 
da i khlaih, bad ban wan tur sha India kam long satia ka 
kam kaba rit ia ka kynthci briew. 

Haba i Miss Thomas i la wan poi ha Shillong ha u bnai 
December, 1897 - ka la long ka Shillong kaba phcr bak Ibak na

bah - bad ka la paw tang ka dur jong ka jingiehnoh i'ingjah 
kaba khraw. Ym don iingmane, ym don iingskul bad baroh ki 
iingbah ki la twa. Ka jingpang iapthem (cholera) ka dang 
khie rasong bad i Miss Annie Williams iba peit ia ka Skul 
Kynthei i la ioh kem ia kane ka jingpang bad i la khlad noh ha 
u bnai August, 1897, i da iehnoh ia i Miss Thomas, ka 
kynthei samla bym pat ju mlien ia kaba kum kane ka apot 
kaba ha kiba bun kan jyn da la long ka bym lah leh ei ei; 
hynrei ka la long pynbah ka jingthew kti ia kane ka kynthei ba 
dang lung.

nang pynch shuh shuh ia ki ban iehnoh ia ka niam rim, bad

u kpa. Hangta hi ka la wan ka jingsawa hapoh jong i, "Wat 
sheptieng, U Kpa jong phi U don hangne." Ka la long da kane

64 



I MISS MARGARET ELIZABETH BUCKLEY 
1887-1941 


1 Miss Margaret Elizabeth Buckley i la long ka mishonari 
nongmaitlynti. I la long kaba nyngkong ban sdang ai jinghikai 
ia ki nongsumar pang (nurses) ha ri Khasi bad Jaihtia. Kane 
ka kam ka dawa ia ka jingtbit, ka jingkut jingmut bad ka bor 
met, ka bor pyrkhat bad ka bor mynsiem kaba khlaih. 

La kha ia i Miss Buckley ha Adfa, Llanwyddelan, 
Montgomeryshire ha ka 8 tarik u February, 1887. La pynsan 
pynrangbah ia i da u kparad jong i, uba long u tymmenbasan ha 
ka Balang Gerizim. I Miss Buckley i la long ka dkhot bapura 
jong ka Balang haba i la dang don kumba 18 snem karta, bad i 
la kynriah ban leit sah sha Cwm-parc ha ka Rhondda Valley, 
hynrei hadien khyndiat por i la wan phai pat sha 
Montgomeryshire bad i la long dkhot noh ha New Mills. I la 
leit pule ia ka kam sumarpang bad i la ioh jingpyntbit ha 
Brownlow Hill Infirmary, Liverpool. Hadien ba i ia shakri kum 
ka district nurse ha Pontardawe i la sngew ia ka jingkhot sha 
ka Lyngkha Mishon, bad i la tyrwa ia la ka jingshakri lyngba ka 
Balang ha Soar, Pontardawe, ha kaba i la long ka dkhot, sha ka 
Foreign Mission Society jong ka Welsh Calvinistic Methodists. 
La pdiang ia i kum ka mishonari ha ka General Assembly ha 
Treorchy ha u June 1919. I la kiew lieng sha India ha ka 27 
tarik u September jong kajuhkasnem. 

I Miss Margaret Buckley i ia pdiang ia ka jingthungkam 
ba shipor ha ka Civil Hospital, Shillong katba i dang ap ia ka 
jingtei ia ka Welsh Mission Hospital ha Shillong. Ha kata ka 


65 



por i la pule ban nang ia ka ktien Khasi. Ka Civil Hospital 
ha Shillong ka long kaba rit kaba don tang iwei ne arngut ki 
nurse. Ia ki nongpang la ri sumar da kiba haiing ha sem la jong. 

U Dr. H. Gordon Roberts uba long u mishonari ha ka 
kam jingpynkhiah bad ka Welsh Calvinistic Methodist Church 
u la ai ia ka jingshakri por thma kum u Civil Surgeon ha ri 
Khasi bad Jaintia. Hadien ba la kut ka thma, u la shimti ia ka 
kam ban tei bad seng nongrim ia ka Khasi Hills Welsh Mission 
Hospital ha Shillong. Ynda la dep tei ia ka Hospital, i Miss 
Buckley i la wan trei noh ha kane ka hospital bathymmai, bad i 
la sdang ia ka kam kaba khia ban ai jingpyntbit ia ki khynnah 
kynthei India ban long ki nurse. Ka long kaba sngewtynnad ban 
pynkynmaw ba hadien ba i la lah pynkhreh ia ka kamra puid ha 
ka hospital thymmai ban pyntreikam noh, i hi i la ioh jingshitom 
na u apendik, bad i la long ka nongpang kaba nyngkong ban 
shah puid ha kane ka hospital thymmai. 

Ki kynthei trai ri kiba la wan ban ioh jingpyntbit kum ki 
nurse ha ka hospital ki wan poi na ki nongrit ba dang lawbah 
lawsan. Ki la ioh tang katto katne ka jingnang jingpule ha ka 
ktien la jong. Katto katne na ki ki la long ki Khristan ka pateng 
kaba nyngkong, bad don kiba long ki khun jong ki kmie ki kpa 
Khristan. Ki la iakynduh jingeh da ki rukom jingngeit bieit kiba 
la snoh ha ki na ka niam bad ki dustur jong ki. 

La katta ruh i Miss Buckley i la jop ha kaba ai ka 
jingpyntbit kaba bniah ha ka ktien jong ki hi bad ha kaba 
pynngam ia ka jingshem mynsiem jong ki ha ka jingiaseng mane 
Blei ha hospital, ka jingpule Baibl bad Skul Pynpaw, kiba la 
iarap ia ki ban long ki sakhi Khristan kiba kordor bad ba 
itynnad. Ka la long ka jingjop kaba palat liam bad ka dei ka 
nuksa jong ka jingaiti baphylla. 


66 



Ha kaba nyngkong i Miss Buckley i la dei ban trei hi da

ka jait dawai kaba pyniap briew. 

Ha ka snem 1925 u khynnah uba dang hynriew snem u la

rem ia ka iing Khasi kaba duk, kaba don hynriew ngut ki khun.

Ha ka 1928 sanngut ki nurse ki la iap namar ka jingpang

Ha u July 1931, i Miss Buckley i la dei ban shah biang ia

Ha ka 1940 ka jingkoit jingkhiah i Miss Buckley ka la hiar

U REV. DAVID EVAN JONES 
1870-1947 


Ka jingiathuhkhana shaphang u Sahep D. E. Jones ka 
long kaba iapom pongkhat. U long u briew u bym pynkhraw 
bad uba buhrieh ia lade, hynrei ka jingshakri jong u ka la 
pynroi katta katta ia ka kam. Ka ri Assam Bashathie, ha ka 
ri India Shatei lam Mihngi, ha kaba u la pynlut ia ka jingim jong 
ka jingshakri jong u, kam da long kaba da tip bha haduh 
mynta. Ka i kum ka bynta ka bym da bit da biang bad kynrum 
kynram jong ka ri India kaba don shaduh Bangladesh, bad 
don ha ki map jong ka ri India ba ym iohi satia ia ka. 

Ka jingheh ka jaka ba ki Mizo ki shong ka long antad kat 
ka jong ka Wales, hynrei ka kham long lum, bad ka kham long 
jrong lyter. Ha ka por u Sahep Jones la tip ia ka kum ka ri 
Lushai, ka kyrteng ia kaba la pynkylla mynta sha ka Mizoram. 
Ka don shibun ki lynter lum kiba noh padun. Ki wah kiba 
tuid stet hapdeng jong ki ki kham bun shaphang shatei. Kham 
niar ban shem ka jaka madan ba lah ban rep kba kum sha ri 
thor. U kba u long ka jingbam kaba kongsan bad ia rep ia u ha 
ki sharing lum. 

Ka Mizoram ka don ka khappud kaba jrong syndah bad 
ka Burma. Sha lyndet ka khappud ba sha mihngi ki don ki lum 
Chin bad kiwei pat ki lum ba jrong jong ka Burma. Kata ka 
bynta ba shaphang sepngi jong ka Burma ka la ju long ka 
jaka shong jong ki Mizo da ki bun pateng bynriew shuwa 
ba kin kynriah kum ki nonghiarthma sha ka jaka kaba ki 
shong mynta. 


75 



Haba kane ka spah snem kaba mynta ka la sdang la 
don tang khyndiat ngut ki Khristan ha ka ri Assam baroh kawei 
(haba pyndonkam ia ka kyrteng Assam kaba mynnor); mynta 
la don palat shi millian ki Khristan Protestant bad shibun ruh ki 
Roman Katholik, ki da pynlong kumba shiphew na ka shispah 
jongka jingdonbriew ha ka ri baroh kawei. Ki mishonari ki 
la wan ban trei hangto na kiba bun ki bynta jong ka 
pyrthei, hynrei kim shym da iabun eh. Shuwa ban pynmih 
noh ia ki mishonari na Assam ha ka 1968, nga antad ba 
don kham bun ki seng mishonari kiba trei ha Japan ban ia 
ki mishonari ha Assam. Hynrei ka jingsan jong ka Balang ha 
Assam ka la long kaba phylla bad khamtam eh ha Mizoram. 

La kha ia u Sahep Jones ha ka IS tarik u February 
1870 bad u la san la heh ha Brynmelyn Farm pyrshah ka 
Wah Dee, hajan Llandderfcl. Kiba ha iing ha sem jong u ki

trei jam ha kata ka nongbah, hynrei u la don ka jingsngew 
kaba jur ba la khot ia u sha ka jingshakri balang. Kumta 
u la Icit pule thioloji ha Bala bad hadien kata u la leit pule 
ha ka University College jong ka Wales, ha Aberystwyth kaba 
dang shu plie thymmai. Hadien khyndiat por u la pdiang ban 
trei ha ka pastorate ha Montgomeryshire (Powys). U la 
sngewkwah ban trei sha shiliang duriaw, bad la pdiang ia ka 
jingtyrwa jong u. Ha ka 1897 ka Foreign Mission jong ka 
Balang Presbyterian ha Wales ka la thung ia u ban leit trei ha 
ka lyngkha mishon kaba thymmai ha ki ri lum jong ka Assam ba 
shathie, ban trei hapdeng ki jaitbynriew riewlum ba ia tip kum 
ki nongmait khlieh briew. 

U la hiar lieng ha Calcutta kaba la don ka jingthrang 
ban ioh khubor shaphang u jumai uba dang jia shen ha 


76 



Assam. U jumai u la pynjulor ia kawei pa kawei ka iing ba 
la tei da ka Welsh Presbyterian Mission ha ri thor Sylhet bad 
ha ri Khasi. Da kaba sngewlyngngoh ia kaei kaba ap shakhmat, 
u Sahep Jones u la bteng ia la ka jingleit lynti da ka rel 
bad da ka lieng, bad hadien katto kalne taiew» ha ka 31 
tarik u August u la poi ha Aizawl, ka jaka kaba u thmu. 

Kumba saw bnai u la sah bad u Sahep Lorrain bad Sahep 
Savidge, ki arngut ki mishonari jong ka Arthington Mission 
(mynta kaba la iasoh bad ka B.M.S.) kiba la don hangto 
naduh ka 1894. Ka la dei ka por ia ki ban leit phai sha 
London bad hadien kata ban leit trei sha kawei pat ka 
jaka, ha Assam ba Shatei. U Sahep Jones u la iasah bad ki ha 
iingtrep ba la thaw da u siej bad sop da u langiap. 

la ka Aizawl ha kata ka por la tip kum ka Fort 
Aijal bad ka long ka jaka shong shipai, kaba don ka ilng 
mishonari sha ka bynta kaba shatei. U Sahep Lorrain bad 
li Sahep Savidgc ki don ruh kawei ka iing kaba rit kaba ki 
ju pynlong kum ka iingmane bad ka skul. Ki la buh nongrim ia 
ka ktien Mizo ha ka thoh ka tar da kaba pyndonkani ia ki 
alphabet kiba iadei bha bad la don katto katne tylli ki kot kiba 
la shon ruh. Hynrei tang khyndiat ngut eh kiba nang ban pule 
bad ym pat don Khristan ba la ioh jingbaptis. Ha ki mishonari 
ka la long ka por kaba da kynjah bha. 

Ki shipai jong ka Kynhun Shipai India ki la don ha Fort 
Aijal ban peit ia ka suk ka sain. Kawei ka jingthmu ka long ban 
pynduh ia ka jinghiarthma man ka por ban ioh khlieh briew. Ki 
Mizo ki la ioh ka jinglute kaba kyrhai na ka daw ka jingbymdon 
ka jingialeh pyrshah ha ki ’per slasha kiba don ha ki jaka ha trai 
lum. 

Ha ka 1871 ki la leit lute ia ka kper slasha ha Alexandrapur 


77 



hajan ka wah Dakeshwari; la ioh ban lute shibun bad la 
rah rub ia ki khlieh briew. Kumjuh kumba ki ju leh, ki

Winchester, kabaukpajongka,unongpyniaid ia kataka ’per 
slasha, u la shah pyniap ha ka jingialeh. 

la ka Mary la set da u Benguaia, u nongialam jong ka 
Sailam haduh shisnem bad shisngi. Shibynta na ka kynhun 
shipa i ki la rung haduh sa khyndiat mail na kata ka shnong 
bad kumta la lah ban iakren pynbeit ia ka jingpyllait noh ia 
ka Mary. Ka la iam kthang pynban haba ka la dei ban 
iehnoh ia kita kiba la kem ia ka, ka da ong, "Ki nongwei 
ki la wan ban shim noh ia nga.” 

Ban pynneh ia ka jingsuk ha Mizoram ka mut ruh ban 
pyniasuk bad kata ka ri. Don ki jingiashun kiba na- duh 
mynhyndai bad ki jingialehthma para riewlum ia kiba dei 
ban pynduh. Ki lynti iaid kim lopg kiba bha bad ka leit ka

mian pa mian. Shisha ki Mizo ki la sngewpyrshah ia ki 
shipai ha kaba nyngkong bad ki la pyrshang ban pynher krad 
ia ki. Ka jingpyrshang kaba khadduh bad kaba shyrkhei tarn 
ka la long ha ka 1891. Kumba saw bnai hadien kata uwei 
u mishonari uba kyrteng u William Williams na ri Khasi u 
la wan jngoh kyr- kieh ia ka Aizawl bad la shon jingmut jur ha 
u ia ki jingdonkam bad jinglong jong kito ki briew. Dei 
kane kaba la ialam hadien ban phah ia u Sahep D. E. 
Jones. 

Hapdeng ki Mizo, uwei na ki nonghikai Khristan ba 
nyngkong eh u dei u Pu Chawnga. U Chawnga u dang long

"Ha ka 1915 katto katne ki Mizo ki la leit trei ha ka ‘Army

dang don ha Aden ba nga iohi nyngkong eh ia ka liengsuih.

ngut ki Khristan ha kata ka kynhun, kumta ki la ai ia nga ia ka 
kyrdan Captain ha ka ‘Indian Army Reserve* kum u officer

nang ban kren ia ka kticn jong ki."

U REV. THOMAS EDWIN PUGH, M.A.(OKon) 
1888-1951 


U Sahep T. E. Pugh u long u khun jong u Sahep bad i Mem 
William Pugh jong ka Talgarth, Brenconshire. La kha ia u ha

sem, khamtam ha kito kiba u la thoh sha u khun jong u u

kdew bunsien sha ka mynsiem ba palei jong ka, ka jingtrei

ha ki jingminot bad ha ki jingjop, bad ha kajuh ka por pat ban 
mad ia ka jingkmen bad jingrhem mynsiem jong ka jingim ha ka 
ling ka sem. 

Ka ju pynshiur ia ki ban iashim bynta ba ka jing- shakri 
ba pura jong ka Balang, ha ka jmgim jong ki skul kiba ki

La kha ia u Sahep Hugh Gordon Roberts ha ka 16 tarik 
uJuly 1885, ukhun jong u David Roberts, u tymmenbasan ba 
la burom shikatdei ha ka Catharine Street Presbyterian 
Church of Wales, ha Liverpool. Na ki khun jong u David, la 
ong ba del u Gordon uba kham shim ia ka jinglong u kpa 
jong u. Ka shnongtrai jong u ka long na Mynydd-y-Gof 
kaba ha Anglesey ha kaba u Dr. David Roberts, u kparad 
tymmen jong u Gordon u la treikam. U Dr, David Roberts u 
don hynhiew ngut ki khun shynrang, laingut na ki ki la 
mih noh na Anglesey. Na kine lai, ar ngut na ki ki la leit 
sha Manchester ha kaba uwei ha ka ia kaba biang u la 
kylla long u Lord Mayor. U la don u khun, u Dr. Fred T. 
Roberts uba la leit sha Tientsin (kum u mishonari doktor) ha 
kaba u la khlad noh hangta. Uwei pat, u Sir William 
Roberts, u la long u Professor jong ka kam ai dawai 
(Medicine) ha ka Manchester Medical School, hynrei 
kaba bun na ka jingim treikam jong u ka long ha kaba ai 
dawai ha London ha kaba u la long uwei na ki laingut ki 
paralok ba ia jan tarn jong u Joseph Lister. Uba lai na kine ki 
shipara u long u Thomas Ffoulkes Roberts uba sah ha 
Llanidloes. U la long u kparad jong u Gordon.

Ki jingpyrshang shitom ba la pynlah ban tei ia ka Hospital 

Ha ka 1913, ka Sorkar Assam kam shym mynjur ia ka 
jingthmu jong ka Mishon ban shna hospital ha Shillong - ym 
don jingpuid briew bad ym donkam hi ruh namar ba lah don

'Civil’ ban pyniapher na ka ' Military’. Ki mishonari ha ri Khasi 
ki la iai bteng ban iakren ia kane ka kam, bad ban iatai bha 
shaphang ka jaka ban tei. Ha kaba khatduh, ki la mynjur lang ia 
ka jingai jingmut u Dr. Roberts ba ka jaka kan dei ha Jaiaw, 
ka bakut jong ka Sor, khnang ban ym don jingeh lada kwah

jingkyrpad jingiarap hakhmat ka Sorkar ha ki daw : 

'1. ba kane ka hospital ka long na ka bynta ka ri Khasi 
baroh kawei, 

2. ba ki Khasi, kumba ka long, kirn da sngewraon ban 
leit sha ka hospital Sorkar, 

3. ba ngi kum ka MLshon ngi donkam la ka jong ka 
hospital ha kaba ngi lah ban phah ia ki parabangeit jong ngi 
bad ia kiwei pat na ki bynta baroh jong ka ri, 

4. ba ngim lah ban ai jingpyntbit ia ki nongkhleh dawai 
(compounder) ha ka rukom kaba thikna lymda ngi don ka 
hospital’. 

(Ki compounder ki dei ki jait nongtrei, kiba ngi lah ban 
khot ki medical assistant, kiba nang ban khlch ia ki dawai, 
ban sopspaih ia ki jingmong bad iarap 1cm naphang ia ki 
nongpang. Ha ki katto katne ki jaka ki long tang ma ki kiba ju 
khmih ia ki nongpang bad sumar ia ki ha ki dispensari ba 
hyndai. Ha ka jingshisha ym pat don compounder kiba ioh 
jingpyntbit ha ka hospital ha baroh kita ki snem. Dei ruh ban 
buh jingmut ba ym shym don kano kano ka jingong shaphang

ka hospital ha u January 1939. Ki la kiew lieng sha Calcutta ha 
kaba kut jong uta u bnai ban trei ha ka Khasi Hills Welsh 
Mission Hospital ha Shillong haduh u bnai May 1969. La thung 
Senior Medical Officer ha ka jaka u Dr. H. Gordon Roberts ha 
ka snem 1942. U la trei kum u Sub Dean, Faculty of Medicine, 
University of Liverpool naduh 1969 haduh 1976.
